<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Why Qwen Code?</h2><a id="user-content-why-qwen-code" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Why Qwen Code?" href="#why-qwen-code"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Agentic out of the box</strong> — Auto-Memory, Auto-Skills, SubAgents, Agent Teams, and MCP. Dynamic workflows, zero setup.</li>
<li><strong>Open-source, inside and out</strong> — The framework and the Qwen models are open-source. They evolve together. No vendor lock-in.</li>
<li><strong>Multi-protocol</strong> — Supports OpenAI, Anthropic, Gemini, and Qwen APIs. Any third-party provider or local model (Ollama / vLLM). Switch at runtime.</li>
<li><strong>Beyond the terminal</strong> — IDE plugins, Desktop app, daemon mode, SDKs, and IM bots (Telegram / DingTalk / WeChat / Feishu).</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-tip" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-light-bulb mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 1.5c-2.363 0-4 1.69-4 3.75 0 .984.424 1.625.984 2.304l.214.253c.223.264.47.556.673.848.284.411.537.896.621 1.49a.75.75 0 0 1-1.484.211c-.04-.282-.163-.547-.37-.847a8.456 8.456 0 0 0-.542-.68c-.084-.1-.173-.205-.268-.32C3.201 7.75 2.5 6.766 2.5 5.25 2.5 2.31 4.863 0 8 0s5.5 2.31 5.5 5.25c0 1.516-.701 2.5-1.328 3.259-.095.115-.184.22-.268.319-.207.245-.383.453-.541.681-.208.3-.33.565-.37.847a.751.751 0 0 1-1.485-.212c.084-.593.337-1.078.621-1.489.203-.292.45-.584.673-.848.075-.088.147-.173.213-.253.561-.679.985-1.32.985-2.304 0-2.06-1.637-3.75-4-3.75ZM5.75 12h4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5ZM6 15.25a.75.75 0 0 1 .75-.75h2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75Z"></path></svg>Tip</p><p dir="auto">Qwen Code is actively iterating on itself — using its own agent and models to file issues, submit PRs, review code, and run tests. Powered by the community, driven by AI.</p>
</div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation</h2><a id="user-content-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation" href="#installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Linux / macOS:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl -fsSL https://qwen-code-assets.oss-cn-hangzhou.aliyuncs.com/installation/install-qwen-standalone.sh | bash"><pre>curl -fsSL https://qwen-code-assets.oss-cn-hangzhou.aliyuncs.com/installation/install-qwen-standalone.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> bash</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Windows:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="irm https://qwen-code-assets.oss-cn-hangzhou.aliyuncs.com/installation/install-qwen-standalone.ps1 | iex"><pre>irm https:<span class="pl-k">//</span><span class="pl-c1">qwen-code-assets.oss-cn-hangzhou.aliyuncs.com</span><span class="pl-k">/</span>installation<span class="pl-k">/</span><span class="pl-c1">install-qwen</span><span class="pl-k">-</span>standalone.ps1 <span class="pl-k">|</span> iex</pre></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Restart your terminal after installation to ensure environment variables take effect.</p>
</blockquote>

<summary>NPM / Homebrew</summary>
<p dir="auto"><strong>NPM</strong> (requires <a href="https://nodejs.org/" rel="nofollow">Node.js 22+</a>):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm install -g @qwen-code/qwen-code@latest"><pre>npm install -g @qwen-code/qwen-code@latest</pre></div>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="brew install qwen-code"><pre>brew install qwen-code</pre></div>
</details>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">How to Use Qwen Code</h2><a id="user-content-how-to-use-qwen-code" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How to Use Qwen Code" href="#how-to-use-qwen-code"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Mode</th>
<th>Command</th>
<th>Use Case</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><strong>Interactive</strong></td>
<td><code>qwen</code></td>
<td>Terminal UI with rich rendering, <code>@file</code> references, slash commands</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Headless</strong></td>
<td><code>qwen -p "..."</code></td>
<td>Scripts, CI/CD, batch processing — no UI</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>IDE</strong></td>
<td>—</td>
<td><a href="https://qwenlm.github.io/qwen-code-docs/en/users/integration-vscode/" rel="nofollow">VS Code</a>, <a href="https://qwenlm.github.io/qwen-code-docs/en/users/integration-zed/" rel="nofollow">Zed</a>, <a href="https://qwenlm.github.io/qwen-code-docs/en/users/integration-jetbrains/" rel="nofollow">JetBrains</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Desktop</strong></td>
<td>—</td>
<td><a href="https://github.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/releases/tag/desktop-latest">Qwen Code Desktop</a> — GUI for macOS, Windows, Linux</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Daemon</strong></td>
<td><code>qwen serve</code></td>
<td>Shared agent session over HTTP+SSE (ACP). Multiple clients, one agent. <em>(experimental)</em> <a href="https://qwenlm.github.io/qwen-code-docs/en/users/qwen-serve" rel="nofollow">Docs</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>SDK</strong></td>
<td>—</td>
<td><a href="/QwenLM/qwen-code/blob/main/packages/sdk-typescript/README.md">TypeScript</a>, <a href="/QwenLM/qwen-code/blob/main/packages/sdk-python/README.md">Python</a>, <a href="/QwenLM/qwen-code/blob/main/packages/sdk-java/qwencode/README.md">Java</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>IM Bot</strong></td>
<td><code>qwen channel</code></td>
<td>Connect to Telegram, DingTalk, WeChat, or Feishu</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<details>
<summary>SDK example (Python)</summary>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import asyncio

from qwen_code_sdk import is_sdk_result_message, query


async def main() -&gt; None:
    result = query(
        &quot;Summarize the repository layout.&quot;,
        {
            &quot;cwd&quot;: &quot;/path/to/project&quot;,
            &quot;path_to_qwen_executable&quot;: &quot;qwen&quot;,
        },
    )

    async for message in result:
        if is_sdk_result_message(message):
            print(message[&quot;result&quot;])


asyncio.run(main())"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">asyncio</span>

<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">qwen_code_sdk</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">is_sdk_result_message</span>, <span class="pl-s1">query</span>


<span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">main</span>() <span class="pl-c1">-&gt;</span> <span class="pl-c1">None</span>:
    <span class="pl-s1">result</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">query</span>(
        <span class="pl-s">"Summarize the repository layout."</span>,
        {
            <span class="pl-s">"cwd"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"/path/to/project"</span>,
            <span class="pl-s">"path_to_qwen_executable"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"qwen"</span>,
        },
    )

    <span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-s1">message</span> <span class="pl-c1">in</span> <span class="pl-s1">result</span>:
        <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-en">is_sdk_result_message</span>(<span class="pl-s1">message</span>):
            <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">message</span>[<span class="pl-s">"result"</span>])


<span class="pl-s1">asyncio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">run</span>(<span class="pl-en">main</span>())</pre></div>
</details>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Capabilities</h2><a id="user-content-capabilities" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Capabilities" href="#capabilities"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If you know Claude Code, you already know Qwen Code — and then some. We've put significant effort into <a href="https://github.com/wenshao/codeagents/blob/main/docs/comparison/qwen-code-improvement-report.md">bringing Qwen Code to feature parity with Claude Code</a>, improving both breadth and reliability across the board.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Ecosystem</h2><a id="user-content-ecosystem" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Ecosystem" href="#ecosystem"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/releases/tag/desktop-latest"><strong>Qwen Code Desktop</strong></a> — Official desktop app for macOS, Windows, and Linux</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/iOfficeAI/AionUi"><strong>AionUi</strong></a> — A modern GUI for command-line AI tools including Qwen Code</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/Piebald-AI/gemini-cli-desktop"><strong>Gemini CLI Desktop</strong></a> — A cross-platform desktop/web/mobile UI for Qwen Code</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/openclaw/acpx"><strong>🦞 Qwen Code Claw</strong></a> — Let other agents (Claude, Codex, etc.) delegate coding tasks to Qwen Code via ACP. Paste this prompt into your agent:</p>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Use Qwen Code as a coding agent. First, fetch and read this skill reference:
https://raw.githubusercontent.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/refs/heads/main/.qwen/skills/qwen-code-claw/SKILL.md
Then follow the instructions inside to install, authenticate, and use Qwen Code via acpx for all coding tasks."><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>Use Qwen Code as a coding agent. First, fetch and read this skill reference:
https://raw.githubusercontent.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/refs/heads/main/.qwen/skills/qwen-code-claw/SKILL.md
Then follow the instructions inside to install, authenticate, and use Qwen Code via acpx for all coding tasks.
</code></pre></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://github.com/modelstudioai/cli"><strong>Aliyun Model Studio CLI</strong></a> — Official CLI for Aliyun's AI platform (<code>bailian-cli</code>). Extends Qwen Code with image/video generation, knowledge retrieval, app orchestration, and model deployment</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Contributing</h2><a id="user-content-contributing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Contributing" href="#contributing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Contributions are welcome! See <a href="/QwenLM/qwen-code/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md">CONTRIBUTING.md</a> for guidelines.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Acknowledgments</h2><a id="user-content-acknowledgments" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Acknowledgments" href="#acknowledgments"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This project was originally based on <a href="https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli">Google Gemini CLI</a> v0.8.2. We gratefully acknowledge the Gemini CLI team's excellent work. Starting from Qwen Code v0.1, we stopped syncing with upstream and began independent development as a multi-protocol, multi-platform agent framework with deep integrations for Qwen models and beyond.</p>
